from content_shell.filter:

1843c3a4adea122f3de4172ca0f474c0a73ea56e [ diff ] 
[iOS] Move tab_switcher EG test disable annotations to expectations

Migrates the `FLAKY_`, `DISABLED_` and `MAYBE_` method prefixes and the
in-body `EARL_GREY_TEST_DISABLED` gates in tab grid, tab groups, shared
tab groups, pinned tabs and tab strip EG tests into
`ios/testing/test_expectations.txt`, following the team rule (flaky ->
`[ Pass Failure ]`, always fails -> `[ Failure ]`, takes down the run ->
`[ Skip ]`).

- Every migrated test gets a `[ Pass Failure ]` line with the tags of
  its original scope, so it can't turn the bots red.
- Tests that failed every run locally also get a strict `[ Failure ]`
  line for the verified config, e.g. the six `TabGridTestCase` drag and
  drop tests on iPad simulators and `testActivityLabel` /
  `testTabSwitcherBadge` on real iPads.
- Three multi-window tests take down the whole run on iPad simulators
  (app and runner crash, or the run stops), so they get `[ Skip ]`
  there. For `testReopenGroupFromAnotherWindow` and
  `testCreateGroupFromTabInAnotherWindow` the `[ Skip ]` lines go after
  the existing gardening lines, because the last matching line wins.
- Verified on iOS 27: 100 runs per test on iPad (A16) and iPhone 16
  simulators, 50 runs per test on real iPad 9th Gen and iPhone 16 Pro.
  Tests that passed every run stay listed here and will be re-enabled
  in separate CLs.

TAG=agy
CONV=8d8bd63e-8f05-48c1-8d69-c6a4484db52d

tree
0af3d04a995ac937070fdfcdd31b2209e44bffc8
parent
5a75e83f268958b943e3b15c32d867ae292a7f56 [ diff ] 
[blink] Apply --blink-settings through WebSettingsImpl setters

Values passed on the command line through --blink-settings were written
straight into Settings by the generated Settings::SetFromStrings(), on
every WebPreferences update. Settings that are routed through
DevToolsEmulator (pointer and hover types, max touch points, script
enabled, ...) therefore lost their emulation override whenever
WebPreferences were pushed while emulation was active.

Move the generated SetFromStrings() into WebSettingsImpl. It converts
the string value and calls the same-named WebSettingsImpl setter, so
emulator-routed settings keep going through DevToolsEmulator, just like
ApplyWebPreferences(). Settings::SetFromStrings() is removed, together
with the converter include in settings_base.cc.

WebSettingsImpl gains private-to-Blink setters, named after the setting,
for the settings whose WebSettings setter is missing, named differently
or takes a different type. The existing public setters call into those.

9455c5466bba1819668810a749e8288b7374cb38
parent
48f40c4571ff8c75018cd44af5f416cb3e143d2f [ diff ] 
Add prompt_min_staleness_days Finch param for in-app updates

The flexible in-app update discovery snackbar is shown as soon as
Google Play reports an available update. Users with Play auto-update
enabled usually receive the update within a few days on their own,
so prompting them adds friction without reducing the patch gap.

Add a prompt_min_staleness_days parameter to the InAppUpdateFlow
feature. The discovery prompt is shown only when
AppUpdateInfo.clientVersionStalenessDays() is at least this value.
When the threshold is positive and Play does not report staleness,
the prompt is skipped. The default of 0 keeps current behavior.

The restart snackbar and the app menu "Update Chrome" entry point
are not gated, and --force-in-app-update bypasses the threshold.

TAG=agy

omnibox: Drop idle shared WebUI popups under memory pressure
📋 Metadata

1812171d1451cafe8b494894c742e2c12a63a624

author
Achuith Bhandarkar <achuith@chromium.org>

tree
22be03f782ff7cffdd5b6890d0f37ac83c77cf03
parent
c2341876497d97f0ef810ebcdde911721832e20c [ diff ] 
omnibox: Drop idle shared WebUI popups under memory pressure

With OmniboxWebUIPopupShareAcrossWindows, a profile keeps one popup of
each kind loaded once a window has used it, even while no window shows
it. On low-memory devices that page, and the popup renderer process that
exists only for it, is memory the system may need back.

Make OmniboxPopupWebUIPool a stateless base::MemoryConsumer behind a new
param, drop_idle_on_memory_pressure, on by default on ChromeOS. Under
critical pressure (or moderate, with drop_idle_on_moderate_pressure) the
pool destroys the idle popups. Shown or busy popups stay, and the next
window that needs a popup loads a new one.

The classic or full popup of the browser window that was activated last
is kept until it has been hidden for active_window_popup_idle_timeout:
the user is likely to open it again soon, and pressure that persists
would otherwise reload it on every use. The timeout is a minute on
ChromeOS, where memory matters more than the first open after a break,
and infinite elsewhere. OmniboxPopupUI records when the page was last
hidden, so the time moves with the page. AI Mode popups are always
dropped.

[WebApp] Fix ChromeOS PreventClose tabbed browsertest flakiness

PreventCloseTestBase did not wait for WebAppProvider subsystems in
SetUpOnMainThread or suppress WebAppRunOnOsLoginManager startup on
ChromeOS, causing delayed OS-login launches to open unexpected windows
and steal focus during tabbed web app tests. Wait for provider
readiness, skip automatic OS-login startup, clear policies in
TearDownOnMainThread, and re-enable both tabbed PreventClose
browsertests on ChromeOS.

2ff0cb925d94d33a7867f0ed08ba3b1c2e69cb3b

tree
55cec253dab9d3ec8df2e027e1ae3e9a1121f6c7
parent
039633ce2f628ea1fca5c91924b07d526134313f [ diff ] 
[browser_actuator] Add idle session eviction and dynamic session cap

TransportSessionRegistryImpl caps concurrent sessions using a
function-local static in GetMaxConcurrentSessionsLimit() (default 3) and
only removes sessions when an explicit CloseSession command arrives. It
also accepts arbitrary non-empty session_id strings without a length
bound.

Caching kMaxTransportSessions.Get() in a function-local static prevents
Finch parameter updates and ScopedFeatureList overrides in tests from
taking effect after the first call. In addition, if prior sessions are
abandoned without receiving CloseSession, the registry remains full and
rejects new sessions for the rest of the profile's lifetime.

This CL reads kMaxTransportSessions dynamically (updating the default to
10), adds a Finch-configurable kTransportSessionIdleTimeout (default 30
minutes) to evict idle sessions before enforcing the cap, and bounds
session_id length to 128 bytes.

Bug: 525542155, 578792925

tree
c22ddd13bfa6185d30ebb628a5e8fd625b9a7747
parent
b2ee828a82d29e327ed161e2f9172f7273e07504 [ diff ] 
[webapps] Only CHECK IsErrorPage() after offline navigation commits

DefaultOfflineWebContentsObserver is created in
TrackOfflinePageVisibility when the alternative error-page override is
built, which happens before the failed navigation commits. If the
WebContents is destroyed or the navigation is aborted before it
commits, DidFinishNavigation runs for an uncommitted navigation where
IsErrorPage() is false, tripping the IsErrorPage() check and falsely
starting the visibility timer.

Pass the NavigationHandle into TrackOfflinePageVisibility so the
observer records the pending error-page navigation id, ignores
non-primary-main-frame and uncommitted navigations, and deletes itself
without logging metrics if the error page never commits. The
CHECK(navigation_handle->IsErrorPage()) now runs only after the tracked
navigation has committed.

Fixed: 575489700
Bug: 575489604
Test: interactive_ui_tests --gtest_filter=All/WebAppOfflinePageTest.*

ash: Restrict chrome/common/chrome_paths.h in DEPS to tests

No production code under //chrome/browser/ash uses
chrome/common/chrome_paths.h any more. Only tests and test support code
do.

- Remove unused #include "chrome/common/chrome_paths.h" from 10 test
  files, and drop the DEPS entries that existed only for them.
- Move "+chrome/common/chrome_paths.h" in //chrome/browser/ash/*/DEPS
  from include_rules to specific_include_rules for the tests that still
  use it. This way checkdeps prevents new production dependencies.

chrome/browser/ash/login/test/DEPS is unchanged, because the directory
contains only test code.

[browser_actuator] Bound consecutive kBufferFull stream reconnects
📋 Metadata
commit
d4d1101acbe80a7a973e98e2fb68da8c6726e968
[ log ]
author
Tommy Nyquist <nyquist@chromium.org>
Sat Nov 07 10:59:46 2026

Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Nov 07 10:59:46 2026
tree
cf4aff4b7fc81991224bd4352f5667d79c939f64
parent
76c9abdd9158da8a6ec07b4f8b8b3e20042aa978 [ diff ] 
[browser_actuator] Bound consecutive kBufferFull stream reconnects

When DownstreamMessageSequencer::AddMessage() returns Error::kBufferFull
because 16 out-of-order messages are buffered across a missing sequence
gap, TransportChannelImpl::OnStreamMessage() calls ReconnectStream() so
the server can replay from last_seen_sequence_number. However, it does
not cap how many consecutive times a session can trigger kBufferFull
reconnects without advancing its sequence cursor.

Because ReconnectStream() calls Disconnect() followed immediately by
Connect(), ProtoStreamClient resets consecutive_failed_attempts_ to 0
and reconnects with zero delay. If the server does not replay the
missing sequence number on reconnect, every subsequent message for that
session overflows the still-full reorder buffer and triggers an
unthrottled reconnect loop.

This CL tracks consecutive kBufferFull overflows per session (resetting
the counter whenever a message is dispatched) and tears down the session
after kMaxConsecutiveBufferFullErrors (3) failed replay reconnects,
matching the existing threshold for consecutive invalid sequence
numbers.

Bug: 525542155, 578792925

[browser_actuator] Advance sequencer on close and require type_url

TransportSessionImpl::ProcessDownstreamMessage() checks
HasTerminatingControlCommand() before routing messages through
DownstreamMessageSequencer, and updates last_seen_sequence_number_
directly without advancing the sequencer's cursor. In addition,
DownstreamMessageSequencer accepts sequence_number == INT64_MAX and
TransportSessionImpl::HasExpectedTypeUrl() treats an empty Any.type_url
as valid.

Because ControlCommand::kCloseChannel disconnects the stream without
destroying registered sessions, registering or removing another session
reconnects the stream while the session remains alive. Updating only
last_seen_sequence_number_ leaves sequencer_.last_seen_sequence_number()
behind, so duplicates at or below the close sequence number are not
dropped and the next consecutive message on a reconnected stream is
buffered as a gap forever. Accepting sequence_number == INT64_MAX also
overflows the next_expected calculation
(last_seen_sequence_number_ + 1), and an empty type_url bypasses
protobuf envelope type verification.

This CL validates that sequence_number is in [0, INT64_MAX) and that
each payload carries a non-empty matching type_url before handling
terminating commands, and advances DownstreamMessageSequencer's cursor
when dispatching terminating commands so session and sequencer state
stay synchronized.

[browser_actuator] Scrub large probe payloads in SessionStreamRecorder

`SessionStreamRecorder` retains recorded downstream and upstream session
messages in memory for the lifetime of the profile and exposes them to
live observers and `chrome://browser-actuator-internals` exports.

Synthetic `TransportProbe` (`ProbeRequest` and `ProbeResponse`) payloads
can be up to 10 MB per message, which would accumulate unbounded
diagnostic heap memory across load-test sessions if stored verbatim.

Scrub `TransportProbe` `ProbeRequest` and `ProbeResponse` payloads
larger than 128 bytes on ingest into a freshly allocated UTF-8-safe
summary string that preserves the original byte length and `PROBE:...`
header prefix while releasing the multi-megabyte buffer immediately.

c5c0942aa1f1601c47e01619617bb467f1d5bde4 [ diff ] 
[browser_actuator] Decode control and probe protos in stream recorder

`SessionStreamRecorder` serializes recorded downstream and upstream
session envelopes via generated `ToValue()` helpers for
`chrome://browser-actuator-internals` snapshots and JSON session
exports.

Because `proto_payload.value` is a raw `bytes` field, recorded
`ControlCommand` (`StartSession`, `CloseSession`, `CloseChannel`) and
`TransportProbe` (`ProbeRequest`, `ProbeResponse`) messages appear only
as opaque base64 strings in live diagnostic snapshots and exported JSON
dumps.

Attach structured `decoded_control_command` and
`decoded_transport_probe` dictionaries alongside `proto_payload` when
serializing `PayloadType::kControl` and `PayloadType::kProbe` entries
whose `type_url` matches the expected proto and whose wire bytes parse
cleanly.

dc1fd279b8f0a36bdecca4036e2052b190825bee [ diff ] 
[browser_actuator] Support delayed ProbeRequest responses

`ProbeTransportHandler` currently handles immediate `ProbeRequest`
replies synchronously and drops requests that specify `delay_ms > 0`,
because `TransportSessionImpl` clears `dispatching_sequence_number_`
once synchronous message dispatch unwinds.

Delayed probe responses must preserve the originating downstream
`responding_to_sequence_number` across interleaved messages, cancel
pending timers on session teardown, and bound in-flight delayed count
and payload bytes across overlapping requests on the same session.

Wire `GetDispatchingSequenceNumber` and `SendProbeReply` callbacks from
`TransportChannelImpl` into `ProbeTransportHandlerFactory` (keeping
`public/transport/` unchanged), schedule `delay_ms > 0` replies via
`PostDelayedTask` guarded by `WeakPtr`, and enforce atomic per-session
`kMaxProbeResponses` and `kMaxTotalProbeResponseBytes` in-flight
budgets.

[browser_actuator] Add ProbeTransportHandler for immediate probes

`PayloadType::kProbe` (`TransportProbe`) messages are routed separately
from lifecycle `ControlCommand` messages, while
`TransportSessionImpl::SendUpstreamMessage` currently only populates
`responding_to_sequence_number` for synchronous `PayloadType::kControl`
replies.

Immediate probe requests (`delay_ms == 0`) need a dedicated
`ProbeTransportHandler` registered on `TransportChannelImpl` that
validates request bounds and emits deterministic `ProbeResponse`
replies correlated to the dispatching sequence number, whereas delayed
probe responses (`delay_ms > 0`) require channel-level sequence
callbacks and an in-flight session memory budget in a follow-up change.

Add `ProbeTransportHandler` and `ProbeTransportHandlerFactory`
(`FactoryId::kProbe`) to handle immediate `TransportProbe::kRequest`
messages via `ValidateProbeRequest` and `SendUpstreamMessage`, populate
`responding_to_sequence_number` for synchronous `PayloadType::kProbe`
replies in `TransportSessionImpl`, and drop requests with non-zero
`delay_ms` without partial replies until delayed scheduling lands.

[browser_actuator] Add ProbeRequest validator and payload verifiers

`:probe_payload` generates deterministic request and response payload
strings, while incoming `TransportProbe.ProbeRequest` messages arrive
over the wire with caller-controlled response counts, payload sizes,
and delays.

Before `ProbeTransportHandler` dispatches `ProbeRequest` messages or
tests construct and check round-trip probe replies, transport needs
shared validation of per-request safety caps and byte-for-byte payload
verification helpers.

Add `ValidateProbeRequest` (enforcing the 256-byte ID, 10 MB payload,
16-response, 20 MB aggregate response, and 30 s delay limits),
`BuildProbeRequest`, `VerifyProbeRequestPayload`, and
`VerifyProbeResponsePayload` to `:probe_payload`.

1482e81fd71ba16236ef7b7585ba7877d9741ff4

d078d38f8aa25c936fa4c56c1fd149b402ec4746 [ diff ] 
[browser_actuator] Add deterministic probe payload generator

End-to-end transport load probes send `TransportProbe.ProbeRequest` and
`TransportProbe.ProbeResponse` payloads of caller-specified byte
lengths across the streaming downstream channel and HTTP POST upstream
channel.

Without a shared deterministic payload generator, the client and server
cannot synthesize exact-size ASCII payloads from matching seeds or
embed human-readable headers for debugging without endpoint drift.

Add `:probe_payload` (`probe_payload.h` / `probe_payload.cc`) to
generate deterministic FNV-1a and `SplitMix64` Base32 request and
response payloads up to 10 MB with self-describing `PROBE:...` headers
when at least 8 entropy characters fit, pinned against cross-repository
golden test vectors.

bfaa823e2acad615deee8f4147fb9e93369deedb [ diff ] 
[browser_actuator] Add TransportProbe wire proto and PayloadType::kProbe

End-to-end transport load probes measure latency, throughput, and
multi-response fan-out across the browser actuator channel, and the
server-side transport protocol defines a standalone `TransportProbe`
message routed under `PayloadType::kProbe` (tag 5) rather than
overloading lifecycle `ControlCommand` messages.

Chromium's `transport_messages.proto`,
`actuator_downstream_message.proto`, and `PayloadType` mappings do not
yet define `TransportProbe` or `PayloadType::kProbe`, blocking
client-side probe payload generation, `ProbeTransportHandler` dispatch,
and internals recording.

Mirror `TransportProbe` (`ProbeRequest` and `ProbeResponse`) and
`ACTUATOR_{DOWNSTREAM,UPSTREAM}_PAYLOAD_TYPE_PROBE` (tag 5) from the
server-side transport protos, and wire `PayloadType::kProbe` and
`FactoryId::kProbe` across payload type mapping, metrics, upstream
client, and `SessionStreamRecorder`.

Bug: 525542155

Ash: Migrate ONCCertificateImporterImplTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

ONCCertificateImporterImplTest now waits for the certificate import's
completion callback before reading the NSS slots. The callback follows
the storage task and key-nickname updates, so TestFuture preserves the
success assertion without draining unrelated work. A single-thread task
environment replaces the manually pumped task runner.

Ash: Migrate NearbyConnectionsManagerImplTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

In NearbyConnectionsManagerImplTest, flush the payload listener pipe to
deliver all three updates before checking for control-frame bytes. The
two successful updates must be handled even though the first failure
destroyed their shared listener. Wait for the subsequent disconnect
request on the Nearby Connections pipe so any unintended cancellation
also reaches the mock before the test ends.

Ash: Migrate clipboard history policy test off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

ClipboardHistoryWithMockDLPBrowserTest now waits for the policy fake to
reject the clipboard read before checking that no text was pasted. A
blocked read does not send the notification used to confirm successful
pastes, so the test observes the rejection directly instead of draining
the task queue.

796898bdffca2d427eccd9a96d15d863121d5dbe [ diff ] 
base: Expose logging::GetLogFileFullPath() on ChromeOS

logging::GetLogFileFullPath() returns the path of the log file set by
InitLogging(), but is available only on Windows. Make it available on
ChromeOS, too.

This is used by ChromeOS to compute the log file path in the user
session log directory on redirecting logs, instead of re-computing the
original log file path from the command line by Chrome specific logic.

[macOS PWA notifications] Limit notification recovery bubble frequency

Track per-app auto-open counts in AppShimRegistry (controlled by the
kAppShimNotificationAttributionMaxAutoOpenBubbleCount FeatureParam,
defaulting to 5) and reset the counter when macOS notification
permission becomes kGranted or the app is uninstalled from all profiles.

Once the per-app limit is reached, stop auto-opening the OS notification
recovery / pre-prompt bubble while still showing the expanded
"Notifications off" chip label.

Ash: Migrate CellularSetupServiceTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

CellularSetupServiceTest now waits for the StartActivation reply using
the helper's existing QuitClosure. The reply binds the carrier portal
handler before quitting, so activation tests receive the handler before
sending delegate and portal updates without draining unrelated tasks.

[Content] Add a high-priority UI queue for subresource loader proxies

When an extension or content blocker (using either `webRequest` or
`declarativeNetRequest`) is installed, `RenderFrameHostImpl` creates a
`WebRequestProxyingURLLoaderFactory` on the browser UI thread that
intercepts every subresource request in the frame. Previously, all Mojo
receivers inside `WebRequestProxyingURLLoaderFactory` and its
`InProgressRequest` for subresource factories bound to the default UI
thread task queue (`BrowserTaskType::kDefault`). Under UI thread
congestion (e.g., queued `DidCommitSameDocumentNavigation` /
`replaceState()` IPCs, tab model observers, or omnibox callbacks during
page load), subresource `CreateLoaderAndStart`, `OnBeforeSendHeaders`,
`OnHeadersReceived`, and `OnReceiveResponse` IPCs were delayed behind
unrelated default-queue work.

This CL introduces `BrowserTaskType::kSubresourceLoaderProxy` mapped to
`BrowserTaskPriority::kHighPriority`, gated behind a new `base::Feature`
(`kSubresourceLoaderProxyTaskQueue`), and passes its task runner via the
existing `navigation_response_task_runner` parameter on
`ContentBrowserClient::WillCreateURLLoaderFactory` when creating a
network-backed `kDocumentSubResource` factory for an outermost main
frame. By default, this is restricted to visible `WebContents` (via
`kSubresourceLoaderProxyTaskQueueVisibleOnly`) and excludes prerendered
`FrameTree`s (via `kSubresourceLoaderProxyTaskQueueIncludePrerendering`)
so invisible background prerenders do not preempt default-queue UI work
for the primary page.

`WebRequestProxyingURLLoaderFactory` already binds its primary factory
receiver, `TrustedURLLoaderHeaderClient` receiver, and per-request
`URLLoader` and `URLLoaderClient` receivers to
`navigation_response_task_runner` when non-null. This CL additionally
binds cloned `URLLoaderFactory` receivers (`Clone()`) and per-request
`TrustedHeaderClient` receivers (`OnLoaderCreated()`, including CORS
preflights) to `navigation_response_task_runner` for
`kDocumentSubResource` factories.

To preserve the practical ordering for cross-document navigations that
`DidCommitNavigation()` and `DidFinishNavigation()` run on the UI thread
before the new document's first subresource `CreateLoaderAndStart()` is
processed (which `extensions/` relies on for frame lifecycle state and
`allowAllRequests` rules), when a subresource `URLLoaderFactory` is
created for a committing `NavigationRequest`, the
`kSubresourceLoaderProxy` task runner is wrapped in a
`base::DeferredSequencedTaskRunner` that is started immediately after
`DidFinishNavigation()` completes. Subsequent post-commit tasks posted
to that runner bypass the deferral and execute directly on
`kSubresourceLoaderProxy`.

Same-document navigations (both renderer-initiated such as
`history.pushState()` / `replaceState()` and browser-initiated such as
fragment or same-document history navigations) reuse the existing
document's subresource `URLLoaderFactory` and continue committing via
`BrowserTaskType::kDefault`. Under UI-thread congestion, a subresource
request issued immediately after a same-document navigation commits in
the renderer can therefore be processed before
`DidCommitSameDocumentNavigation()` updates URL-dependent per-frame
state (such as `declarativeNetRequest` `allowAllRequests` rules).

Ash: Migrate NetworkStateTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

NetworkStateTest now waits for the fake cellular device's initial
properties to reach NetworkStateHandler. The posted Shill reply wakes
the sequence, and GetDeviceState exposes the device only after that
update, so setup no longer needs a broad task drain.

🌺 Revert "[Payments] Batch 11 payments javatests after HistogramWatcher migration"

This reverts commit 3a59df89ec03f17c91eddf004c219104330b6b29.
Culprit: 3a59df89ec03 - "[Payments] Batch 11 payments javatests after HistogramWatcher migration" (refs/heads/main@{#1738335}, https://crrev.com/c/8697075)

Reason for revert: consistently fails android-14-arm64-rel.

Mechanism: Commit 3a59df89ec03f17c91eddf004c219104330b6b29 (r1738335,
https://crrev.com/c/8697075) added @Batch(Batch.PER_CLASS) to
PaymentRequestPaymentAppUiSkipPreloadTest and replaced
FreshCtaTransitTestRule with AutoResetCtaTransitTestRule
(fastAutoResetCtaActivityRule()). When the 5 payment app UI-skip preload
tests run in a shared ChromeTabbedActivity batch instead of destroying
the activity after each test, a PaymentRequestService /
PaymentRequestSpec instance created via PaymentRequestSpecJni.create
(PaymentRequestService.init -> PaymentRequestSpec.<init>) is not
destroyed before BaseJUnit4ClassRunner.onAfterTestClass runs
LifetimeAssert.assertAllInstancesDestroyedForTesting(). This throws
BaseJUnit4ClassRunner$AfterClassException
(LifetimeAssert$LifetimeAssertException: Object of type
org.jni_zero.JniUniquePtrImpl was not destroyed after test completed),
marking all 5 tests in PaymentRequestPaymentAppUiSkipPreloadTest as
FAIL.

All 5 tests in
org.chromium.chrome.browser.payments.PaymentRequestPaymentAppUiSkipPreloadTest
(testPayViaDelayedFastBobPay, testPayViaDelayedSlowBobPay,
testPayViaFastBobPay, testPayViaSlowBobPay, and
testTwoPaymentsAppsWithTheSamePaymentMethodName) have failed in 2/2
recent builds (#20915, #20916) of android-14-arm64-rel, every attempt in
every one of them. This is not flake: it reproduces on every run, so it
blocks the CQ for every unrelated CL that touches this suite. The
regression appears at refs/heads/main@{#1738335}.

Example failing runs:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-14-arm64-rel/20915
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-14-arm64-rel/20916

To run/deflake this test on CQ before relanding, add the following
footer to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.android:android-14-arm64-rel|chrome_public_test_apk

Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the failure is
understood and covered; this revert is about restoring CQ throughput,
not about the merit of the change.

Original change's description:
> [Payments] Batch 11 payments javatests after HistogramWatcher migration
>
> Batch the remaining 11 non-@DoNotBatch PaymentRequestTestRule classes:
> - Migrate cumulative RecordHistogram.getHistogramValueCountForTesting()
>   assertions on PaymentRequest.Events2 and CanMakePayment histograms to
>   per-test HistogramWatcher expectations.
> - Keep FreshCtaTransitTestRule on PaymentRequestFreeShippingTest while
>   batching it with @Batch(Batch.PER_CLASS), so NightMode is configured
>   before activity launch rather than recreating a live reused
>   ChromeTabbedActivity (crbug.com/562594074).
>
> Together with removing the 2s sleep, runtime across these 11 classes
> (63 active tests on x64, 19 on Desktop) drops from 8.8m -> 3.9m (-56%)
> on Android x64 CI (8.1m -> 3.9m, -52% on CQ) and 2.9m -> 1.4m (-51%)
> on Android Desktop.
>
> Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:android-desktop-x64-rel
> Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.android.desktop:android-desktop-x64-rel-15-tests|chrome_public_test_apk

Ash: Migrate DriveFsPinningManagerTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

DriveFsPinningManagerTest.DropQuery now binds the test query to a local
Mojo receiver and waits for its disconnect. The reply releases the query
after Stop() invalidates the manager's weak pointer. This exercises the
late response before returning without draining unrelated tasks.

c907f911ac5ae88ff42ea596587a57897207757b
parent
7136a3f5eb85a7c4d3fb40645593710a67c8dd3a [ diff ] 
[PA] Add capacity-unlimited option to SchedulerLoopQuarantine

Add a capacity-unlimited configuration flag and capacity_unlimited_
field to SchedulerLoopQuarantineBranch. When capacity_unlimited_ is
true, GetCapacityInBytes() returns std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()
(used by IsQuarantineTarget()), and Quarantine() skips the capacity
check, purge, and random slot shuffle since all entries are purged at
the end of a task.

As a temporary compatibility workaround for existing Finch
configurations, make GetSchedulerLoopQuarantineConfiguration() treat
branch-capacity-in-bytes == INT_MAX (2147483647) as
capacity_unlimited = true when capacity-unlimited is omitted.

https://pinpoint-dot-chromeperf.appspot.com/job/102385ab990000
https://pinpoint-dot-chromeperf.appspot.com/job/1438fb4f990000

Bug: 582527150

0f1b3e3476d90941f9e083a0272c8163942fafe1 [ diff ] 
[TabStrip] Add pinned tab drag-and-drop integration tests

Add cross-window pinned tab drag-and-drop integration tests to
TabDragDropTest and MultiTabDragDropTest covering:
- Dropping a pinned tab over a pinned tab (lands at target index in
  pinned section and remains pinned).
- Dropping a pinned tab (or multiple pinned tabs) over an unpinned tab
  (clamped to the end of the destination pinned section).
- Dropping an unpinned tab (or multiple unpinned tabs) over a pinned tab
  (clamped immediately after the destination pinned section).
- Dropping a mixed selection of pinned and unpinned tabs across windows
  (pinned tab clamped to pinned section, unpinned tab placed in unpinned
  section).

Bug: 493931798
Test: TabDragDropTest, MultiTabDragDropTest

Extensions: Use JniUniquePtr in TabContextMenu

Passing native pointers as raw long values bypasses MiraclePtr
protections and type safety across the JNI boundary.

Migrate ExtensionTabContextMenuBridge to hold a JniUniquePtr to the
native ExtensionTabContextMenuBridge C++ object. The C++ object is
owned by its Java peer and destroyed via JniUniquePtr's deleter when
ExtensionTabContextMenuBridge is destroyed.

EdgeToEdge: Use JniRawPtr in TopScalpSceneLayer

EdgeToEdgeTopScalpSceneLayer stored its C++ peer as a raw long,
bypassing JNI Zero type safety and BackupRefPtr quarantine protection
for the subclass alias while base SceneLayer owns the native object.

Migrate mNativePtr to JniRawPtr<NativeEdgeToEdgeTopScalpSceneLayer>
created via jni_zero::MakeRaw(), pass JniPtr to native methods, and
release the handle in destroy() before super.destroy() deletes the
native peer.

JavascriptTabModalDialog: Adopt JniRawPtr

JavascriptTabModalDialog stores its native
::javascript_dialogs::TabModalDialogViewAndroid pointer in raw long
field mNativeDialogPointer. Passing raw integer handles across the JNI
boundary bypasses type checking and lifetime validation, risking
use-after-free bugs when the C++ counterpart is destroyed.

Migrate mNativeDialogPointer to JniRawPtr with a package-private
JniTypeToken mapped to ::javascript_dialogs::TabModalDialogViewAndroid
via @JniType. Construct the borrowed reference via jni_zero::MakeRaw,
release it via JniRawPtr.release() on teardown, and pass JniPtr to
@NativeMethods calls.

424585603633871615dd8dac1d5fc690a7a29e7e [ diff ] 
[Bookmarks] Relocate CombinedBookmarksView to //chrome/browser/bookmarks

This CL moves `CombinedBookmarksView` and its unit tests from the
desktop-only `//chrome/browser/ui/bookmarks` directory to
`//chrome/browser/bookmarks`.

Why this is necessary:
* `CombinedBookmarksView` implements the `bookmarks_api::BookmarksView`
  interface (from `//components/browser_apis/bookmarks`), providing a
  unified view of local and account bookmark hierarchies with synthetic
  root node and UUID mapping.
* Although `CombinedBookmarksView` contains purely model-level logic and
  has zero UI dependencies (interacting solely with `BookmarkModel` and
  `ManagedBookmarkService`), it was originally placed in
  `//chrome/browser/ui/bookmarks`.
* `//chrome/browser/ui/bookmarks/BUILD.gn` contains an explicit
  `assert(!is_android)` guard, preventing Android targets from compiling
  or referencing any classes in that directory.
* Moving `CombinedBookmarksView` into `//chrome/browser/bookmarks` (the
  canonical cross-platform home for bookmark model utilities and
  factories) allows it to be shared with Android for the WebUI Bookmarks
  Mojo service implementation, while adhering strictly to Chromium's
  directory layering and platform boundary rules.

Changes:
* chrome/browser/bookmarks/BUILD.gn:
  * Add `combined_bookmarks_view.h` to `source_set("bookmarks")`.
  * Add `combined_bookmarks_view.cc` to `source_set("impl")`.
  * Add `combined_bookmarks_view_unittest.cc` to
    `source_set("unit_tests")`.
  * Add `//components/browser_apis/bookmarks:impl` dep.
* chrome/browser/ui/bookmarks/BUILD.gn:
  * Remove `combined_bookmarks_view.{h,cc}` and unit test.
* chrome/browser/ui/bookmarks/bookmarks_service_feature.cc:
  * Update `#include` path to point to `//chrome/browser/bookmarks/`.
* chrome/browser/bookmarks/combined_bookmarks_view.{h,cc}:
  * Update header guards and `#include` paths.
* chrome/browser/bookmarks/combined_bookmarks_view_unittest.cc:
  * Update `#include` paths.
  * Update test assertions using
    `bookmarks::TestBookmarkClient::IsDesktopFormFactorByDefault()` to
    account for desktop vs. mobile permanent folder visibility rules on
    Android builders (including in
    `AccountPermanentFoldersSignInAndSignOutEvents()`, which landed
    upstream while this CL was in flight).

No production code behavior change.

295379f8a5aa51fca561d6c8f31b3cb9addca000 [ diff ] 
[winhttp] Clean up ScopedWinHttpProxyInfo

- Move the members that allocate or free memory out of the header, into
  a new scoped_winttp_proxy_info.cc. Chromium style allows inline
  definitions and snake_case() names only for cheap accessors. The
  GlobalAlloc() and FreeString() helpers become functions local to the
  .cc file; GlobalAlloc() is renamed to AllocGlobalString(), so that it
  no longer shadows ::GlobalAlloc.
- Rename receive() to Receive(), and set_proxy() and set_proxy_bypass()
  to SetProxy() and SetProxyBypass(), since they are not simple
  accessors.
- Rename IsValid() to has_proxy(). It only checks whether a proxy server
  is set, so a "no proxy" result is not invalid.
- Remove proxy(), which has no callers.
- Document get() and the moved-from state.
- Pass the proxy info to SetProxyForRequest() by const reference.
- Add the header to the `public` list of the target, since the public
  headers proxy_configuration.h and network_fetcher.h include it.
- Test has_proxy() for a default object and for a bypass-only object.

No behavior change.

Bug: None

[winhttp] Fix leaks in ScopedWinHttpProxyInfo

295379f8a5aa51fca561d6c8f31b3cb9addca000

author

Sat Nov 07 05:01:32 2026
tree
6822e03ef793346786f82b33c8d135d2293464a6
parent
b891bc8873a76a71404285877820723a40a6a2f3 [ diff ] 
[winhttp] Fix leaks in ScopedWinHttpProxyInfo

ScopedWinHttpProxyInfo could overwrite the proxy strings it owned
without freeing them, which leaks the GlobalAlloc-ed buffers, in three
places:
- operator=(ScopedWinHttpProxyInfo&&), which also cleared the strings
  of the object on self move-assignment.
- set_proxy() and set_proxy_bypass(), when a string was already set.
- receive(), when the structure already held strings.

No current caller does any of these: nothing move-assigns the class,
ProxyConfiguration::DoGetProxyForUrl() calls each setter once on a new
object, and it calls receive() again only after WinHttpGetProxyForUrl()
fails. The fix makes the class safe to reuse.

Free the owned strings before replacing them, and make self
move-assignment a no-op. The setters allocate the new string first and
keep the old one if the allocation fails. receive() now returns a
cleared structure. Use std::exchange in the move operations, which
also resets the access type of the moved-from object. State in the
class comment that the object owns the strings.

Add unit tests for the move operations, the setters, and receive().
The tests check the resulting state; no test can detect the leaks
themselves, since LeakSanitizer does not run on Windows.

Bug: None

2084ee03a917cf564708aa8857e2d09a3ead9b83 [ diff ] 
Content: Drop raw ptr in ContentUiEventHandler

ContentUiEventHandler is stored in WebContentsImpl's UserDataHost while
its C++ peer is owned by WebContentsViewAndroid without a native
teardown callback to Java. Caching a raw C++ pointer in Java risks stale
dereferences after WebContents destruction and would trigger
LifetimeAssert failures when WebView relies on garbage collection to
reclaim UserData entries.

Remove mNativeContentUiEventHandler from ContentUiEventHandler, guard
event forwarding on WebContents.isDestroyed(), and resolve the C++
handler from WebContentsViewAndroid on each JNI call.

55e3dda257c9b927c8ff45b21c2b5824d8956fba [ diff ] 
Feed: Own ReliabilityLoggingBridge via JniUniquePtr

Passing C++ pointers across the JNI boundary as raw long values bypasses
type safety and smart pointer protections, which risks use-after-free
bugs when native objects are deleted or accessed improperly.

Migrate mNativePtr in FeedReliabilityLoggingBridge to JniUniquePtr so
JNI Zero tracks the native peer lifetime and cleans up the native bridge
on Java destroy(). Return JniPtr from getNativePtr() and pass it
directly to FeedSurfaceRendererBridge to eliminate raw pointer casts.

0ad7168ab961a027cc780bca6a358e1db8315cf4

3a59df89ec03f17c91eddf004c219104330b6b29 [ diff ] 
[Payments] Batch AndroidPaymentAppFinder and ManifestDownloader tests

Batch AndroidPaymentAppFinderTest and PaymentManifestDownloaderTest
with @Batch(Batch.PER_CLASS) and fastAutoResetCtaActivityRule().

These were unbatched because they appended --ip-address-space-overrides
for the test server's host:port in @Before. The network service parses
that switch only once per process, while each test gets a server on a
new random port, so every test after the first one in a batch hit
local network access blocking. Use "127.0.0.1:0=public" instead (port 0
matches any port) via @CommandLineFlags.Add, so the override covers all
test servers in the batch.

AndroidPaymentAppFinderTest also gives each finder its own
TestServerDownloader. A finder destroys its downloader when it
finishes, but its manifest verifier can still be downloading in the
background, e.g. to refresh manifests cached by earlier tests in the
batch. With one downloader per test, a second finder in the same test
could hit the destroyed downloader.

ExpandablePaymentHandlerTest stays unbatched:
testNavigateBackWithSystemBackButton fails most runs when batched.

Runtime of the 63 tests in these two classes:
- android-x64-rel: 4.62m (CQ baseline) / 5.24m (CI) -> 0.76m (-84%)
- android-desktop-x64-rel: 5.22m (CI baseline) -> 0.95m (-82%)

Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:android-desktop-x64-rel
Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.android.desktop:android-desktop-x64-rel-15-tests|chrome_public_test_apk

parent
b89d66224683d4e3d975639c6cf5e3ed4103cf71 [ diff ] 
[FSA] Match sensitive-path blocklist rules case-insensitively

This CL updates ChromeFileSystemAccessPermissionContext to evaluate
sensitive-path blocklist rules and home directory dotfile checks
case-insensitively.

Previously, while suffix blocklist rules compared path components
case-insensitively, regular blocklist rules and the home directory
dotfile check relied on case-sensitive path comparisons. When saving a
new file that does not yet exist on disk, path normalization cannot
canonicalize the on-disk casing, allowing case-variant paths such as
_VIMRC or VIMFILES to bypass sensitive-path checks on case-insensitive
filesystems.

Fixed: 581785712

cf4afe01e4ead98e50a2dab541d19b94f86e6dd3
parent
79658068e8fb6fa76672c93330fad5fdd66ebe10 [ diff ] 
Content: Use JniUniquePtr in WebContentsObserverProxy

WebContentsObserverProxy stores its native
::content::WebContentsObserverProxy address in a raw Java long field.
Passing untyped integer handles across the JNI boundary bypasses
compile-time type checking and ownership enforcement, exposing callers
to use-after-free bugs if the peer is used after teardown.

Declare a package-private NativeWebContentsObserverProxy JniTypeToken
mapped to ::content::WebContentsObserverProxy and hold the Java-owned
peer in a JniUniquePtr<NativeWebContentsObserverProxy> allocated via
jni_zero::MakeUnique. Destroy the native peer via destroy().

Also clear Java WebContentsObservers in TabImpl.destroyWebContents when
slow shutdown defers native WebContents destruction, and stop
GlobalNightModeStateController before clearing UI_THEME_SETTING in
ChromeNightModeTestUtils teardown.

3a1c82053933043396c062287495b82b0baba8fa

8bc29e34b0f4106165b71a8abd050b777c803938
parent
ecbfb99b392c400f0e0e67db40a3474f457fa59d [ diff ] 
[remoting] Limit Windows curtain-mode desktop resolution to 4K

When connecting to a Windows host in curtain mode (RdpSession) from a
high-DPI display (such as a 5K or 6K Retina display at 2x scale),
UpdateSessionDisplaySettings() can fail and cause Windows' indirect
display driver (rdpidd.dll) to crash with protocol error 0x112f when the
requested virtual desktop dimensions exceed 4K.

Lower kMaxRdpScreenWidth and kMaxRdpScreenHeight from 8192x8192 to 4K
UHD (3840x2160 in landscape or 2160x3840 in portrait). Instead of
clamping width and height independently (which would distort the aspect
ratio), add ScaleSizeToFit() in remoting/base/util and update
remoting/host/win/rdp_client with GetBoundedRdpDesktopSize() and
GetBoundedRdpResolution() to scale dimensions down proportionally, clamp
to the 800x600 minimum, and align to even pixel boundaries. When
dimensions are scaled down to fit within 4K, client DPI is scaled down
by the same factor (with a 96-DPI minimum floor) to preserve the
effective logical desktop dimensions.

0b60f38cc701d77ff038fd3048e778512ff4b50b
parent
3c35d89945f800e3fb6fd5564a10dc9ffae137e9 [ diff ] 
[IndexedDB] Only share connections that match the renderer's target

With IndexedDBConnectionDeduplication, the browser previously shared a
connection whenever the client token had any open connection to the
database. However, the renderer's open request may no longer have a
connection to share (e.g. if the primary open request it piggybacked on
failed, or if the existing connection belongs to a dedicated worker
sharing the document's client token), triggering
`CHECK(shared_connection_target_)` in
`IDBOpenDBRequest::OnOpenDBSuccess` when the browser replies with a null
`IDBDatabase` remote.

Replace `bool request_shared_connection` in `IDBFactory.Open()` with
`int64? shared_connection_id` (the `transaction_id` of the open request
that created the connection the renderer expects to share). On the
browser side, record that `transaction_id` on the `Connection` once
`OpenSuccess()` is sent, and only share when a connection matches both
`shared_connection_id` and the full `BucketClientInfo`.

d53cdbd83ee0946ee925a93ccaf8c498e039a8e9 [ diff ] 
android: Pin Android 17 Desktop AVD creation config to emulator 37.1.11

Pin emulator_package in android_37_desktop_x64.textpb
back from 37.2.12 (Stable) to 37.1.11 (Stable), matching
https://crrev.com/c/8759211.

Emulator 37.2.12 suffers from host Gfxstream OpenGL ES memory leaks
(https://issuetracker.google.com/568480829) and an unlocked mWindows
teardown race during multi-process browser tests, causing exit=87
emulator hangs and disconnects on ci/android-desktop-17-x64-fyi-rel
(e.g. https://ci.chromium.org/b/8665854496225144145). Meanwhile,
emulator 37.2.13 crashes on ProcessCleanupThread in
EglContext::~EglContext() (https://crrev.com/c/8759211).

Emulator 37.1.11 predates both 37.2.x regressions while supporting
GLESDynamicVersion=on, Uwb=off, VirtioMouse=off, VirtioTablet=on, and
swangle_indirect.

tree
bfd4755e091dfc21d3d2723f8f74947a6b5f7ba5
parent
fc8ab5574aa08168521bf3a8defcc7b67359296e [ diff ] 
Clear SVG mask/clip-path caches when the last client is removed

SVG mask and clip-path resources have caches on their layout objects.
Before this patch, these caches stayed populated after the last
referencing client was removed, retaining unused paint records and
paths, and risking stale cached mask output if a nested mask's
display-lock state changed while unreferenced.

Clear the cached mask and clip-path data when the last client is
removed, matching gradients and patterns.

Bug: 576326793
